update_company_Mitsubishiexchangehousebank_currency_stored_liste = {
	effect = {
		clear_global_variable_list = company_Mitsubishiexchangehousebank_privat_bank_stored_currency

 		#dinar
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:stockpiling_dinar_company_Mitsubishiexchangehousebank_fixe > 0

			}
		}
 		#dinar_algerian_dinar
		if = {
			limit = {
				global_var:stockpiling_dinar_algerian_dinar_company_Mitsubishiexchangehousebank_fixe > 0
